 This is the ease involving the bombing of
the Sixteenth Street Baptist Church,
Birmingham, Alabama, 9-15-63, in which four
Negro girls were killed.
 By letter dated 12-7-66, the Department
was advised that 11 microphone surveillances
and 7technical surveillances were used during
the investigation of matter.
 this No pertinent
information ta investigation
 this was obtained
from any of them,
 installations
 These were on prime suspects
Several of the prime suspects in this
 case were
prematurely arrested in the early stages of
this investigation by the Alabama State Highway
Patrol. This has materially hindered the FBI
from obtaining admissible evidence. Results
of our investigation have been furnished to
the Department. There is no indication that
the Department contemplates prosecution in
this matter in the future.
 It appears the
 that Department&#39;s request
is an
 outright instance of afishing
expedition; therefore, the Department will be

 This is the case involving the bombing of the
16th Street Baptist Church, Birmingham, Alabama, September 15,
1963, in which four Negro children attending Sunday School
were killed.

memoranda pertaining to microphone and technical surveillances
used in case

 this
material is suitable for dissemination to the Department.

Bureau or on which

 not taken.
